## c9309bf · 2026-07-10 · feat(decks): lecture-hall legibility — G22 auto-fit gate + def-body 24px + fix 15 squished slides

Root-caused from L18 s31 (and s14 earlier) reading too small in a hall: effective font
size = authored font × the auto-fit scale deck.js applies when content overflows 1080px.
The readability gate (G20) only checks the RAW authored font, so a slide that auto-fit
shrinks below ~0.65 (→ small on the wall) sailed through — and also spammed the pre-flight
"dense" overlay (the "warnings still fire" the user saw).

Generalized fix, three parts:
  • Systemic base font — .def-card .def-body referenced an UNDEFINED --fz-lead token and
    fell back to 1.25rem (20px) in every deck; bumped the fallback to 1.5rem (24px), so the
    def-card explainer on all two-col/definition slides reads bigger. Composition-verified:
    0 overflow (auto-fit absorbs the growth).
  • G22 legibility-gate (_audit/legibility-gate.mjs, wired into site-ci layout bucket) —
    enforces min auto-fit ≥ 0.65 on PROSE slide types. Uses auto-fit (one robust number) not
    per-element px, which is unreliable across bilingual <span lang> nesting. Grandfathered
    baseline like the facts-gate; the baseline is now EMPTY. Self-test included.
  • Remediated the 13 genuinely-squished prose slides (< 0.65) across L09/L10/L11/L12/L17 by
    trimming redundant secondary prose (no numbers/formulas/data touched, both langs kept),
    plus L18 s31/s24 and the L05 (+) i18n nit. Every prose slide now clears 0.65.
  • pre-flight's dense warning is now PROSE-ONLY (figures/refs/stepped types fit-scale by
    design — matches G22 scope), clearing the last L05/L15 figure/refs warnings.

Result: ALL 19 decks pre-flight "no issues". Verified: build 103 · facts 0 · i18n 0 ·
parity/composition/readability 0 · G21 0 · G22 empty baseline · screenshot-checked.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>


## a3224ca · 2026-07-10 · fix(decks): retype 21 slide-type↔content mismatches + G21 enforcement gate

Root-caused from L18 s14 rendering "criminally small": the slide was data-type="e2e"
(a stepped-walkthrough type) but a static formula slide. The e2e type gives none of
data-type="formula"'s 280% math scaling + centred fill, so the equations rendered
~19px on the 1920px canvas. The SAME mistype also made pre-flight warn "has no
.walk-step/.e2e-step children" — the "mass warnings on open" the user saw.

Two sibling classes of the same defect (slide typed X, missing X's required markup),
21 slides across 5 decks — each shipped past every existing gate:
  • 14 STEPPED (e2e/walkthrough) without steps → retyped to their honest type:
    8 widget → viz, 3 formula-stage → formula, 2 table → table/two-col, 1 → two-col.
  • 7 MISCONCEPTION without .misc-statement/.misc-truth → 6 → definition, 1 → two-col
    (they were def-card/twocol slides wearing the interactive-reveal type).
s14 + s28 also trimmed a redundant line/table so the correct layout fits above the
0.65 auto-fit floor. All 5 decks now pre-flight "no issues" (were L08=8, L14=7 warns).

Durable fix — G21 slide-type-gate (_audit/slide-type-gate.mjs, wired into site-ci
static job): a pure-static build gate enforcing both contracts, lifting pre-flight's
runtime checks so a mistyped slide can't reach a deck again. Self-test: 8 cases.

Verified: build 103 pages · facts-gate 0 · G21 0 + selftest · parity/composition/
readability all 0 · every touched slide auto-fit ≥ 0.68, screenshot-checked across
all type classes (formula/definition/two-col/viz/table).

## 993fe6b · 2026-07-08 · Merge: harden Lab 3 + A3 after strict correctness/feasibility audit



## 78da855 · 2026-07-08 · fix(assignments): harden Lab 3 + A3 after a strict correctness/feasibility audit

Adversarial audit (formulas re-derived by hand + a fresh critic pass) found the
specs would leave a student stuck or graded wrongly. Fixes:

Lab 3 (ANN):
- Do not download all ~500K docs: STREAM the documents config + filter to a ~5K
  subset (one source_type ∪ every gold expected_doc_id, so gold is always indexed).
- Fix random_state=20260605 for every k-means / PQ codebook fit (was unseeded →
  non-reproducible tradeoff table).
- PQ codes are ⌈log2 k⌉-bit (not "one-byte"), and the bytes formula is the PACKED
  size — so the k=16 (4-bit) memory sweep actually matches m·log2(k)/8.
- Drop the corpus-specific "PQ recall ~0.70 at m=4" (a SIFT-128 figure); state a
  qualitative ceiling (~0.4–0.8 here, do not target a number).
- HNSW trace: CONSTRUCT a graph where ef=1 is trapped (not stated as guaranteed).
- Note MiniLM truncates long docs (fine — ANN fidelity is vs the same embeddings).

A3 (RAG):
- Corpus subset now UNIONs in every gold expected_doc_id (was source_type+sample →
  silently dropped gold docs → capped recall); STREAM the corpus.
- Pin the answer-fact match rule (cosine ≥ 0.6 under the Lab-2 encoder), reused in
  both the recall-floor and context-recall steps (was unspecified — the headline).
- context precision = MAP DEDUP'd to parent doc_id (per-chunk AP drifted with chunk
  size when qrels are per-document).
- info_not_found: metrics are N/A (0/0); report an explicit abstention metric.
- multi-query merges into ONE ranking by max-similarity so MRR/nDCG are defined
  (a set-union is not a ranking).
- Judge lab: position/verbosity need the local judge over answer PAIRS the student
  builds from gold_answer; only the Goodhart weight-flip is "by hand" (the old
  "fully by hand from provided answers" covered neither and referenced no file).
- Note relevance is BINARY (nDCG gains 0/1); "reuse Lab-1/2 CODE, re-indexed" (not
  the Lab-3 ANN index) and the corpus switch from nfcorpus.

Grade weights unchanged (100 / 60+40). Build green, 119 pages. Dataset schema
verified against the HF datasets-server API (configs documents/questions, split
test, all field names + doc_id↔expected_doc_ids join confirmed).

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>


## a1e7417 · 2026-07-08 · Merge: Lab 3 (ANN by hand) + A3 (RAG retriever + eval) on EnterpriseRAG-Bench (#lab3-hw3)



## b598254 · 2026-07-08 · feat(assignments): add Lab 3 (ANN by hand) + A3 (RAG retriever + eval) on EnterpriseRAG-Bench

Lab 3 "The index at scale" (L9): build IVF, Product Quantization and IVF-PQ by
hand on the Lab-2 dense embeddings; measure the recall / work / memory triangle
vs exact search (nprobe & (m,k) sweeps, the ADC table, a mandatory exact
re-rank), with an optional HNSW greedy-descent trace.

A3 "Build the RAG retriever, then judge it" (L10+L11): chunking sweep + the
recall floor, query-rewriting lift (multi-query / HyDE / RAG-Fusion), RAG metrics
by hand (context precision = MAP; context recall from gold answer_facts), and an
LLM-judge bias lab (position / verbosity / Goodhart-weight flip + swap-and-average
mitigation) — plus a paper essay (Lewis RAG, HyDE, Late Chunking, Lost-in-the-
Middle, RAGAS, LLM-as-judge, ReAct).

Both use EnterpriseRAG-Bench (onyx-dot-app, MIT): expected_doc_ids are the qrels
and gold_answer / answer_facts make context recall & faithfulness hand-computable
(what nfcorpus could not support). Common setup updated (topic; by-hand rules incl.
no faiss/hnswlib/scann/annoy and no RAGAS lib; ANN k-means helper; ERB dataset

## daf0182 · 2026-07-03 · feat(L16-L18): three new deep-dive topics — Late Chunking, Shannon Entropy, The Curved Map

Adds three supplementary lectures to the L13–L15 bar, each = a ~48-slide bilingual (RU/EN)
deck + a trilingual (en/ru/tt) Book chapter + 12 Séréga plates (nano-banana-pro) + a
reproducible stdlib data generator, all gates HARD=0.

L16 "Read the Whole Book First" (Late Chunking, arXiv:2409.04701): chunk-then-embed severs
coreference; embed the whole doc first, then pool per chunk. Toy: gold answer-chunk rank 2→1
(cos 0.52→0.71) as "it"→ACME binds. The two lates (ColBERT interaction vs chunking).

L17 "How Much Is a Letter Worth?" (Shannon Entropy), history-forward: Morse/Vail → Nyquist/
Hartley → Markov-Onegin → Shannon 1948/1951 → the entropy of English (~1 bit/letter, the
guessing game) → source coding floor → cross-entropy/perplexity → IDF = self-information.
Toy: biased coin H=0.811; the dyadic code that ACHIEVES H=1.75.

L18 "The Curved Map" (anisotropy & hubness): embedding spaces are distorted maps — the cone
(random words ~0.99) and hubs (all roads to Rome) make cosine lie. Fix: re-project (center →
whiten W=UΛ^-1/2 → contrastive) + a local-crowding tax (CSLS). Toy: cone cosine 0.90→−0.03,
hubness skew 0.03→2.59.

Infra: gen_l16/17/18.py + data/l16/l17/l18-*.json (reproducible); course.json (19 lectures);
papers.json (+9 citations); gen_images.py (+36 briefs); check_claims deck/book:L16-L18 facts
baselines (numbers come from the generators + cited bench). Build = 94 pages.

## e05fb98 · 2026-06-29 · perf(ci): shard slide-viz (the 12.6min bottleneck) into 2 parallel buckets

slide-viz (G9) was the single slowest browser bucket (~12m38s) and bounded the
whole browser job. Added a `--shard i/n` arg that partitions the per-deck +
per-chapter RENDERING round-robin across n jobs (the fast static color-contract
scan still runs in every shard, so contract coverage is unchanged). Split the
`viz` matrix bucket into viz-a (--shard 1/2 + selftest) and viz-b (--shard 2/2).

Verified locally: shard 1/2 renders 7 decks + 7 chapters, shard 2/2 renders 6 + 7
— together every deck-with-stepped-slides (01–13) and all 14 chapters, no overlap,
both HARD=0, selftest PASS. Expected browser wall-clock ~12min → ~8min.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>


## 004333c · 2026-06-29 · perf(ci): parallelize the 28-min browser-gate job into 3 matrix buckets

The browser job ran 8 heavy Playwright gates SEQUENTIALLY (~28min wall-clock).
Split into 3 parallel matrix buckets (fail-fast:false) so wall-clock ≈ the slowest
single bucket (~slide-viz, ~10-12min) instead of the SUM:
  • viz    → G9 slide-viz
  • probe  → G13 viz-probe · G11 wbw · G10 widget-render
  • layout → G8 responsive · G18 parity · G19 composition · G20 readability
Every gate + selftest runs in exactly one bucket (coverage preserved, verified).
Shared setup (build, Playwright, npm, WebP cache) runs per-bucket but is cached.

Tradeoff: ~3× the build/setup minutes for ~2.3× faster feedback. Independently
revertible if CI dislikes it.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>


## d5bd1e6 · 2026-06-29 · perf(build): content-addressed WebP cache — stop re-encoding 180 plates every build

optimize-images.mjs re-encoded all 179 PNG plates (255 MB) through sharp on EVERY
build (~6.5s local / ~15-25s on a 2-core CI runner), even when no image changed.
Now each plate's WebP is cached by content hash (.cache/webp); an unchanged plate
is a copyFile() instead of a re-encode. Local: 6.46s → 0.32s on a warm cache (20×),
byte-identical output.

Persisted across CI runs via actions/cache (.cache/webp, keyed on the PNG sources
with a rolling restore-key) in all three build jobs — deploy.yml + site-ci {static,
browser}. .cache/ gitignored. Both workflows validate.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>


## fa24fef · 2026-06-29 · fix(issues): RU-layout keyboard shortcuts (#2) + L1 keyword-stuffing example (#6)

#2: deck shortcuts (T tools, F fullscreen, L lang, D theme, V, N, O) didn't fire
on a Russian keyboard layout — matches() only checked e.key ('е' for the T key),
never the binding 't'. Added a codeChar() fallback in deck.js that derives the
Latin letter/digit from the PHYSICAL key (e.code: KeyT→t, Digit5→5), so
single-letter shortcuts are layout-independent. presenter.js/lab.js use only named
keys (Arrow/Escape) — unaffected.

#6: the L1 "not sufficient" example claimed the spam page "java java coffee java
cup java" contains the query "java tutorial" perfectly — but it has no "tutorial".
Changed the query to "java" (which the page is saturated with) so overlap is
genuinely perfect yet the page is still spam. Fixed in deck slide 28 + book beat
03 (en/ru/tt).

#4 (missing nDCG comma) was already fixed in a prior commit — slide 32 shows
"rank 2, g=3" in current code and at session start.

## ab619e9 · 2026-06-25 · feat(gates): G20 readability scale-floor + G13 severe-overlap→HARD — stricter readability guards

The recurring failure the user named — "масштаб, наложения и пересечения" (text too small;
illustrations overlapping/colliding). The existing gates only WARNed on these. This makes
readability ENFORCED, calibrated to ZERO false positives against the live decks + book.

- G20 _audit/readability-gate.mjs (NEW) — renders every deck slide (1920×1080) + every Book
  page; HARD-fails PROSE authored below the legibility floor (deck 11px / book 12px RAW font).
  Floors the RAW --fs-* size, NOT effective px: the decks fit-scale content to the frame by
  design (a diagram renders at ~0.38, a packed body at ~0.9) so effective px swings 6–30px and
  an effective floor false-fires everywhere. Figures/SVG/KaTeX/reveal-answers are excluded —
  figure legibility is G9/G13's domain. Calibrated via --measure; selftest PASS; HARD=0 across
  14 decks + 14 book chapters. (Building it surfaced + fixed an id-collision that had been
  silently limiting the scan to slide 1 of each deck.)
- G13 _audit/viz-probe-gate.mjs — figure-label OVERLAP was WARN-only; now a SEVERE overlap
  (≥50% box cover = a real collision, the "наложение" defect) HARD-fails, while mild crowding
  (30–50%) stays WARN (dense real-data figures carry justified partial overlap). HARD=0 today.
- site-ci.yml: G20 wired (+ --selftest) in the browser job.
- CLAUDE.md: H4 + Definition-of-Done gain a "Readable — scale + no collisions" box (G20 + G13).

overlaps ≥30% across all decks. Both gates HARD=0; both selftests PASS.

## e4ad6af · 2026-06-22 · perf+stability: 3 new gates, CI parallelized + cached, decks untracked

Eight optimizations across stability / codebase / deploy-speed.

STABILITY — turn recurring audit classes into deterministic gates (with --selftest):
- G13 viz-probe-gate (_audit/viz-probe-gate.mjs): the AUDIT-3 forensic probe promoted to a gate.
  Drives EVERY step of EVERY published deck slide (EN+RU) via #/N/k; HARD on collapsed figures,
  broken plates, off-slide elements, bilingual doubling (overlaps/sub-16px = WARN). Catches exactly
  the L8 RU-divider collapse + L11 climb-widget overflow the step-0 visual gate missed. HARD=0 now.
- G14 check_citations.py: every arXiv id cited in a deck/Book beat must resolve in papers.json
  (the stated single source). Round-3 found ~24 missing; arXiv-HARD (DOIs not gated — all recorded,
  noisy to extract). HARD=0 now.
- G15 check_lexicon.py: forbids the recurring RU/TT defect classes — soft-hyphen in Cyrillic, the
  rejected renderings "чанкинг"/"реранкинг", and model/version decimal-commas (GPT-3,5). All four are
  currently 0, so this only blocks RE-introduction. Surfaced + fixed one real stray soft-hyphen in
  widgets/pagerank-power/i18n.json (Багана­лар → Баганалар).
- G16 data-immutability: `npm run build` must not mutate committed data/ (the CI-feasible H3 guard;
  full byte-identity reproduce.sh stays local — its frozen macOS/py3.9 ABI can't run on a Linux runner).

DEPLOY / CI SPEED:
- site-ci split into two PARALLEL jobs (static = pure Node/Python gates; browser = Playwright), so
  wall-time ≈ the slow job, not the sum.
- Cache the Playwright Chromium download (~/.cache/ms-playwright) — was re-fetched every run.
- concurrency: cancel-in-progress on site-ci + ci.yml — superseded runs are cancelled (deploy keeps
  cancel-in-progress:false intentionally).

CODEBASE:
- Untracked the 13 assembled decks Lectures/NN-*.html. They were already in .gitignore (declared
  build output) yet still tracked from an old commit, so every content change re-committed ~half a MB
  of reassembled HTML. Now built fresh by CI + copy-static (published Pages unaffected); a raw checkout
  rebuilds them with `npm run build`.

Verified: all gates HARD=0 (incl. the 3 new + their selftests) · build 76 pages · widget-render-check
HARD=0 · all three workflow YAMLs valid (jobs + concurrency).

## 19f1cd9 · 2026-06-22 · fix(viz): eliminate dense-figure label crowding — GloVe headers, word2vec axis, graphrag edges

Closes the last documented illustration residual (the ~6 partial label overlaps in dense real-data
figures). Each fixed with the right deterministic layout tool, verified by the hardened probe v2.

- L5 s30 GloVe co-occurrence matrix: column headers steepened -60°→-85°. A rotated label's
  axis-aligned bbox width is textW·cosθ + textH·sinθ; at -60° a 6-char word ≈28px > the 22px column
  pitch (→ clipped the next tick). At -85° the footprint collapses to ≈ text-height (~16px,
  word-length-independent), so adjacent headers can never overlap; still leans, still clears the title.
- L5 s16 word2vec loss-strip: the "loss" axis label overprinted the "4.85" top tick. Made it a proper
  rotated vertical y-axis title at the far left (x18, rotate(-90)), fully clear of the tick column.
- L12 graphrag widget: added an auto-layout declutter over the edge-midpoint relation labels (the
  placeLabels idea applied to edge labels — relax any horizontally-overlapping pair apart in y), so
  two offset labels (e.g. "studied at" vs "located in") can never overprint. Re-baselined widget-viz
  (graphrag-only paint-signature drift, visible-counts unchanged, no errors).

VERIFICATION (all green): build 76 pages · probe v2 ALL decks = 0 overlaps / 0 collapsed / 0 broken-WebP
/ 0 off-slide / 0 bilingual-doubling (the dense-figure residual class is now empty) · facts-gate
HARD=0/WARN=0 · slide-viz --strict HARD=0 · layout PASS · widget-render HARD=0 (re-baselined) ·
wbw 65/0 · offline-deck pageerrors=0/nonLocal=0 · responsive 0. No data/ touched → H3 holds.

## 1970440 · 2026-06-22 · perf(pages): build-time WebP for deck/book plates — 245MB → 30MB artifact

The slow GitHub Pages deploy was the artifact step: docs/ shipped 213MB of
full-res PNG illustrations (161 plates, up to 5.9MB each). Convert them to
WebP at build time and rewrite the references in the build output only —
sources stay untouched.

- scripts/optimize-images.mjs (run last in `npm run build`, after copy-static):
  converts docs/Lectures/assets/img/**/*.png → .webp (sharp, q88, longest
  side ≤1920px, downscale-only), deletes the PNGs, and rewrites every
  `assets/img/…png` ref in built HTML → `.webp` (deck-relative `…?v=` and
  book absolute-base alike; cache-bust query preserved). Fails the build if
  any `.png` plate reference survives.
- sharp pinned as a devDependency (CI-portable encoder; bundles libvips —
  no apt/cwebp needed on ubuntu-latest).

SOURCE UNTOUCHED: Lectures/assets/img, _research/gen_images.py, the image-gate,
and the committed offline standalone decks all stay full-res PNG (file://
archival rendering unaffected). Only the served docs/ artifact is WebP.

Measured: images 212.9MB → 17.8MB (12×); docs/ 245MB → 30MB (8×).
Verified: build 76 pages · 782 refs rewritten in 88 HTML files · 0 dangling
.png · all 159 distinct .webp refs resolve · deck 00 6/6 plates render
(file://) · responsive-gate HARD=0 across all book pages · facts-gate
HARD=0/WARN=0 (source unchanged).

## e8f97fe · 2026-06-18 · fix(viz): clear 3 text-over-graphic overlaps in L9/L10 dynamic figures

A targeted visual audit of every stepped figure in L9/L10 (rendered at
its final step, light + dark) surfaced three real overlaps that slide-viz
misses by construction — it scores text-vs-text (IoU/overprint) and
box-vs-viewBox OOB, but is blind to text printed UNDER a fill and to
sibling labels sharing one baseline:

- recall-curve (L9 s19 ef-sweep, s28 nprobe-sweep): the x-axis title and
  the cost-legend rendered ~4px apart on one baseline and overlapped
  horizontally (both span most of the width). Drop the legend to its own
  line (captionTop +18) and grow the frame.
- rag-control-flow (L10 s46 CRAG): the three grade-threshold tags
  (correct ≥0.7 / ambiguous 0.3..0.7 / wrong <0.3) were centred at their
  arrow midpoints, which are near-coincident under the single grade
  diamond → unreadable mush. Place each tag above its OWN branch box
  (columns at 0.18/0.5/0.82·W are far apart) and add a background halo
  (paint-order: stroke) so the vertical centre arrow is masked, not bisected.
- chunk-size-sweep (L10 s18): the per-row labels (overlap=N / N chunks) at
  x=20 ran to ~x230 but the first chunk bar starts at x=61 and is drawn on
  top → labels clipped. Move each to a single-line caption above its strip.

pre-existing colour-void on the IVF query dot (untouched, intentional).

  registry and gate so a briefs-only edit still triggers the check.

## 262a0f2 · 2026-06-12 · fix(G10): make the visual-drift baseline PER-PLATFORM-aware (un-break ubuntu CI)

The T2 paint-signature baseline is platform-specific (font metrics + sub-pixel
rendering differ across OSes, like a golden screenshot), but it was frozen on
macOS and wired into site-ci on ubuntu-latest -> the cross-platform render
drifts and G10 HARD-fails on CI (a regression T2 introduced when it committed a
cross-platform pixel baseline).

Fix: record _meta.platform on --update-baseline; on compare, when the baseline's
platform != the runtime platform, report drift as INFORMATIONAL (not gated) --
the baseline HARD-gates only on its own OS (local dev / a same-OS runner). The
RUN checks (mount/console/setStep/empty) still gate EVERY platform.
WIDGET_VIZ_FORCE=1 forces the comparison anywhere. Baseline regenerated with
platform=darwin (sigs byte-identical -- only the +platform field changed).

V: local darwin run still HARD-gates (0 drift, exit 0); --selftest PASS; the
ubuntu CI run will now pass G10 (cross-platform drift informational; RUN checks
green).

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>


## 774e4f6 · 2026-06-12 · refactor(gen): genlib.write_json — single serialisation for gen_l3/gen_l4 (byte-identical, H3)

gen_l3 (10) + gen_l4 (6) all used the identical inline form
`(DATA/"X").write_text(json.dumps(OBJ, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")`.
Hoisted to genlib.write_json(path, obj) — one definition for that format. The
other generators use a DIFFERENT serialisation (no trailing newline /
encoding=utf-8 / 3-place rounding) and keep their inline call, per genlib's
"only the provably-identical helpers" rule.

H3 byte-identity PROVEN: re-ran gen_l3 + gen_l4 under the vendored toolchain
(/usr/bin/python3 3.9 + pylibs cache) -> git diff data/ EMPTY. facts-gate
HARD=0 WARN=0 + selftest PASS (data unchanged -> provenance intact).

## 3f619f8 · 2026-06-12 · perf(G9): slide-viz loads each deck/chapter ONCE (per-slide/beat -> per-unit), 515s->380s

The deck driver reloaded the whole deck per stepped slide (runDeck, 55x goto) and
the book driver reloaded the chapter per beat per theme (runBook, 74x goto). Both
now load each UNIT once and drive all its figures on the shared page:
  - runDeckGroup: load deck once, navigate every stepped slide via `#/N`. The
    `#/N` hashnav resets the slide to step 0 on re-entry (deck.js render():
    keepStep false unless the hash carries `#/N/M`, M>0) -- the same mechanism the
    per-slide driver already used to capture both themes on one load, so slides
    never leak step state across navigations or theme passes.
  - runBookChapter: load chapter once per theme, drive every beat (each owns its
    window.__figs[beat] + #fig-<beat>; independent step state, no leakage).
Page loads: 129 -> 21 (deck 55->7, book 74->14). Per-figure capture code is
byte-for-byte the old drivers; only the page is shared.

PARITY PROVEN (A/B, same machine, full --strict over 92 figures):
  - report body BYTE-IDENTICAL: 461 report lines (every === header, every
    visible-count/step, every defect/clean line) diff to 0 between OLD and NEW.
  - verdict HARD=0 WARN=0 unchanged; --selftest PASS (detectors untouched).
  - timing: 515.39s -> 379.93s (-135.5s, -26%).

The 12 softmax bars on L6 slide-48 ("InfoNCE loss = 0.1191") were 12 hardcoded
<rect height> magic coords with NO gate tying them to data. They are in fact

(verified: max |height - softmax*220| = 0.12px across all 12). New provenance
check provenance_l6_nce binds them:

      loss == -ln(p+) per checkpoint (pins logits <-> p+ <-> loss);

      .nce-slide, matched by x-coord (925/1075/1225/1375), 3 per x in checkpoint
      order. Editing a bar OR drifting the trajectory logits without the other
      now HARD-fails.

makeScale render-rewrite is NOT done: L6 has no DeckLayout in its head, and a
static->JS rewrite of a fixed teaching figure is high-risk/low-value; the gate
delivers the "numbers from data / drift unauthorable" goal with ZERO render
change (the deck HTML is byte-identical).

each FIRE; healthy silent. facts-gate HARD=0 WARN=0.

  - book 06 climb-contrastive x2: the anchor 'cat' dot (circle.cs-anchor r=8,
    ~15px, near-black) flagged a "void hole" -- it is the figure's most
    prominent MARK, not a hole.

    fill=var(--bg-inset)@0.5, no class) flagged "fill ~ background".

    content; an anonymous bare shape ~ bg is a generic backing panel,

## 948e05b · 2026-06-11 · T2: widget visual-regression baseline — paint-signature drift gate (G10)

widget-render-check (G10) now proves a widget renders the SAME thing, not just
that it RUNS. Per (widget·beat, theme, step) it records a paint signature —
visible count + a hash of per-mark (tag + coarse frame-relative box + fill /
stroke / stroke-width / paint-order; coords Math.round(/6), the slide-viz
bucketing) — and HARD-fails on DRIFT vs a committed baseline
(_audit/baselines/widget-viz.json: 37 widgets, 332 signature rows, BOTH themes
since light/dark resolve different fills). --update-baseline re-freezes after a
reviewed, intentional visual change.

Determinism: reducedMotion:'reduce' + a transition-freeze stylesheet make the JS
camera tween (_plot-util cameraTo dur:620, the pca-rotate s4 flake) and CSS
transitions land at their unique REST state, so the coarse signature is
timing-independent — proven 0 drift across two independent processes (the writer
that froze the baseline and a separate verifier process).

--selftest adds 4 visual-regression fixtures alongside the existing RUN ones:
identical-re-render -> silent; missing-element (3->2 marks) -> fire; dropped
halo/stroke with count UNCHANGED -> fire (exactly the regression T3 needed a
human eye for); collapsed step (setStep no-op) -> fire. The RUN checks (S1-S4:
mount-missing / console-error / setStep-throw / empty-render) are unchanged.

Metric: widget-steps under a visual baseline 0 -> 332. HARD=0; RUN checks intact.
CI: G10 comment in site-ci.yml updated (same command now runs the drift check).

## 3c9c4f9 · 2026-06-11 · T1-1d-ii/-i.b: widget textHash + per-element OOB own-frame (kills last 2 FP classes)

Two more detector refinements that clear the remaining false-positive classes
the project-wide scan surfaced (book widgets + multi-panel OOB):

1d-i.b — STEP-PROG whole-figure textHash (WIDGET targets only):
  CAPTURE emits textHash of the figure's root.textContent;
  detectStepProgression counts a textHash change as progress, but ONLY for
  type==='widget'. Book widgets animate by rewriting values/captions in their
  own <div>s (pagerank-power 0.3333->0.2148, course-map narration) that the
  capture's label selector does NOT match, so the per-label signature couldn't
  see them (probed: captured labels are the static graph/map text). Decks are
  EXCLUDED — their chrome step-counter/stage-name ticks every step and would
  blind the dead-step/everything detector; decks keep relying on the captured
  is-current signature (already proven on the 19 e2e slides).

widgets. Triage (screenshot + e2e.js/source trace): 19 type=e2e deck slides
+ 3 book widgets are FALSE-POSITIVES, not real defects.

Root causes:
- e2e walkthroughs (Lectures/js/e2e.js paint()) progress by toggling
  is-current/is-visited classes + rewriting the stage-name; element COUNT is
  flat by design, so the count-only anyProgress (ignoring sigChanged) misread
  it as "everything at step 0".

  animate by rewriting TEXT in place; the signature tracked {key,hi,box} but
  NOT text, so it read as a string of dead steps.

Fix (detectStepProgression + CAPTURE signature):
- signature now folds each label's text -> in-place value/caption updates
  register as a change.
- everything-at-step-0 anyProgress now also counts sigChanged (consistent with
  the per-step dead-step branch which already honoured it).

false-positive fixtures stay silent — B4 class-toggle walkthrough (is-current
advancing, count 6->6->6...), B5 in-place text update (0.890->0.850 rewriting,
geometry fixed). A1-A4 fire, B1-B5 silent, C1-C2 correct -> PASS.

## 64ed572 · 2026-06-11 · G: facts-gate coverage-guard ratchet (no NEW un-gated displayed number)

Closes the verification asymmetry: registration auto-extends via globs,
but facts-gate coverage was a whitelist -> new content could silently
ship an un-gated hand-typed number that drifts.

[G] coverage-guard in check_claims.py (modelled on font-gate's ratchet):
strip tags -> displayed-text surface (SVG geom attrs excluded), extract
grounded decimals (>=2 frac digits; arXiv/dates excluded), count those
not within max(claim-tol,0.001) of a gated value. Per-surface count may
not exceed COVERAGE_BASELINE; a NEW unit (L7) defaults to 0 -> every
number must be gated or justified. Grandfathers 295 current un-gated
numbers (mostly coarse roundings + worked intermediates), forbids growth.

V: empirical proof -- throwaway L7 deck with un-gated 0.7531 -> HARD=1
exit1; with gated 0.1191 -> exit0. facts-gate 190 checks HARD=0 WARN=0;
--selftest fires the ratchet (new-unit L9) + PASS; only check_claims.py
changed; git diff data/ empty; build unaffected.

## 7215cda · 2026-06-11 · token-gate: add --c-* fallback hue-drift check (R9/D) + fix 16 drifted fallbacks (render-neutral)

Closes the "6 semantic --c-* hues drift unchecked" gap. The token-gate stripped var(),
so a var(--c-NAME, #HEX) fallback whose hex != the token's canonical value went
uncaught (the documented case: archflow used var(--c-violet, #7C5CCF) while the token is
#7D5BA6).

- New [D] sub-check in token-gate.mjs: scans tokens/ + Lectures/css/ + widgets/ for
  var(--c-*, #hex) fallbacks and HARD-flags any whose fallback != the token's canonical
  value. Wired into the HARD tally + --selftest (a planted #000000 mismatch must fire).
- Surfaced + FIXED 16 drifted fallbacks. RENDER-NEUTRAL: --c-* are always defined at
  :root, so the fallback is dead code that only renders if the token is absent — making
  it equal the token changes nothing visible. Fixed: archflow.css violet #7C5CCF->#7D5BA6
  (x5) + violet-soft + red-soft (x2); widgets/ranking-metrics/style.css green/green-soft
  (x3). The other 93 --c-* fallbacks already matched.

Verified: token-gate HARD=0, "109 --c-* fallbacks consistent", --selftest PASS.
Render-neutrality confirmed: slide-viz --strict HARD=0 WARN=26 (archflow figures ARE
gated targets) + wbw-check 35/0 — unchanged.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

## 26c052b · 2026-06-11 · Phase 5: wire the Book into the facts-gate (Book prose numbers now gated)

Closes the standing correctness gap (check_claims docstring's own TODO: "When the
Book lands, add its built HTML"). Until now the Book's numbers were UNVERIFIED — a
data/ change that didn't propagate to the Book prose would ship silently.

- load_book(): glob docs/<en>/book/NN/index.html, keyed L<n> (adding book/07 auto-covered).
- book_claims(): 15 flagship Book-prose numbers (L3 idf/BM25/BEIR · L4 nDCG/MRR/MAP ·
  L5 PCA-2D/analogy/GloVe-drop/loss · L6 InfoNCE). Each REUSES the value+tol of its deck
  claim by id (single source — the number is never re-typed); only the anchor differs,
  because the Book renders the same numbers in its own prose/KaTeX (only 7/111 deck
  anchors matched the Book, so Book-specific anchors are required). Anchors are
  context-pinned generic captures, so a drifted number is flagged, not silently missed.
- main(): runs Book [C] claims when docs/ is built (WARN + skip if not — the gate runs
  after npm run build in CI); arithmetic [A] now scans deck + Book in one pass.
- selftest(): a Book chapter with a drifted flagship number must flag DRIFT (new okBK).

Outcome: the 15 flagship Book numbers are currently CONSISTENT with data/ (0 drift) —
the Book was authored carefully; the gate is now a SENTINEL against future drift.

V1: facts-gate 135 checks (was 119; +15 Book + 1 summary), HARD=0 WARN=0, exit 0;
--selftest PASS ([selftest:book] DRIFT in L5 — displayed 99.9 vs data 37.7). V2: build
49 pages. No other gate affected (only check_claims.py changed; decks/Book unchanged).

Extendable: more Book numbers = more rows in BOOK_ANCHORS (L1/L2 + non-flagship
intermediates). H4 strengthened.

The lecture-template-ci workflow (ci.yml) failed at the "Pre-flight corner-case checks" step:
preflight-corner.mjs navigated to http://localhost:8099/Lecture Template.html but NOTHING starts a

(mirroring ci-gate.mjs), closes it at the end, and — since it previously always exited 0 regardless of
results — now EXITS NON-ZERO if the clean template reports errors, any corner-case detector fails to
fire, or the revert isn't clean (a real gate, not a no-op).

offCanvas/demoSyntax/demoBlacklist), clean err=0, PASS, exit 0. All 6 ci.yml gates exit 0
(ci-gate/preflight-corner/archflow-audit/archflow-negative/sequence-audit/budget-audit); site-ci's 10
gates + selftests all exit 0; npm run build clean (49 pages).

## 9794da3 · 2026-06-09 · Fix contrastive double-paint (CSS cascade) + aim slide-viz-gate at all 16 book widgets

The contrastive-space label defect fooled us twice — an independent pass found the real root
cause + why the gate missed it, and this closes both for good.

Root cause: widgets/contrastive-space/style.css had `.cs-pos{stroke:c-green}` / `.cs-neg{stroke:
c-red}` declared AFTER `.cs-pt-lbl{stroke:bg-card}` at equal specificity, so the intended
near-white halo on every neighbour label (dog/kitten/airplane/computer/france) was overpainted
by a saturated 2.5px colored stroke = double-paint, garbled in dark+mobile (anchor "cat", lacking
those classes, was the only clean label — the tell).
- Fix: scope the colored stroke to SHAPES only (circle.cs-pos/neg fill, line.cs-pos/neg stroke,
  arrows); labels keep ONLY the bg-card halo (paint-order:stroke), colour via fill. Computed-style
  now: every label stroke ≈ bg-card, saturation ~0. Lengthened label radial offsets (18→30 / 24→34)
  + widened arrow-clearance (4→8px) so no arrow shaft/head crosses a label. VLM-clean across all 8
  combos (en/ru × desktop/mobile × light/dark), steps 0–3.

Why the gate missed it: the double-paint detector existed but slide-viz-gate's BOOK_TARGETS didn't
include contrastive-space. Extended BOOK_TARGETS 8→16 (contrastive-space + embedding-space,
glove-cooccur, dimred-projection, tsne-steps, attention-e2e, positional-enc, transformer-block) so
the double-paint + overprint detectors now run on every book widget. Selftest C3 (saturated
c-green/c-red 2.5px stroke on text → HARD) + C4 (fixed bg-card halo → silent) prove it catches this
exact class. Tuned 4 newly-surfaced false positives (line stroke-vs-fill, near-neutral tint
collisions via COLLIDE_SAT_MIN 0.20, bar-fill structural exemption, opacity floor 0.2) without
neutering (A3 still fires).

Gates: build clean, slide-viz-gate --selftest PASS, slide-viz HARD=0 across 26 targets (10 deck +
16 book), scroll-step 0, i18n en/ru/tt 100%, responsive 0, facts 0.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

## e1551ac · 2026-06-09 · slide-viz-gate: close the recurring blind spot — double-paint + text-overprint detectors

The independent VLM kept catching two defect classes that BOTH visual-gate and slide-viz-gate
passed: (A) colored-stroke double-paint on stepped SVG <text> (the global
`[data-arch-step].is-arch-current{stroke:accent;stroke-width:4}` rule garbled s38 LayerNorm and
s31 GloVe), and (B) SVG-text-over-text overprint (s30 panel-title over the diagonal column
headers; glove-cooccur headers over the subtitle). Now both are HARD-gated:

- Detector A: flags an SVG <text> with a chromatic stroke (HSV sat ≥0.20, width ≥2px). Halo-aware
  — silent on the legit white/bg outline technique (ΔE(stroke→white|bg) <26, OR paint-order:stroke
  + light stroke, OR -webkit-text-stroke). Severity HARD.
- Detector B: adds a smaller-box-coverage metric (inter/min-area) on top of IoU — a wide title
  burying a narrow header has tiny IoU but high coverage; ≥0.50 different-string ⇒ WARN, ≥0.65 ⇒
  HARD. Cross-namespace (SVG <text> + HTML overlay). Excludes same-string/substring (halos, ticks),
  text-vs-shape, and both-rotated adjacent labels (legible — diagnosed via the rotated-AABB false
  positive). Severity HARD.

--selftest: 12 fixtures (8 prior + 4 new), both directions — accent-garble & title-over-header
fire; white-halo & text-over-own-bg-rect & same-string-shadow stay silent. Current state: HARD=0
across all 18 targets (no residuals; WARN=8 = pre-existing baseline). Already runs in CI as G9.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

## 1b8ab25 · 2026-06-09 · GloVe (#4) + t-SNE (#6) deepened: step-by-step calc + architecture + visual, real numbers

Instructor: "GloVe and t-SNE are explained too superficially — no step-by-step calc, no
architecture, no visual examples." Gave both the full word2vec/PCA treatment across the deck,
the Book, and reproducible data — like the rest of the lecture.

Data (reproducible, /usr/bin/python3):
- gen_l5_glove.py → data/l5-glove.json: real co-occurrence matrix X from a curated mini-corpus,
  the weighting f(x)=(x/x_max)^0.75, and a deterministic numpy GloVe fit (loss 18.04→0.005)
  with worked entries (king·queen: X 0.667, log X −0.406, f 0.131, model −0.408, residual ≈0).
- gen_l5_tsne_math.py → data/l5-tsne-math.json: the full t-SNE pipeline on 9 real GloVe vectors —
  Gaussian p_{j|i} with σ≈2.003 tuned to perplexity 5 (=2^H), symmetrized P, Student-t Q,
  KL(P‖Q)=0.0411, and the gradient.

Widgets (book, scroll-step, progressive, trilingual): glove-cooccur (corpus→X→f(x)→objective→
factorization) and tsne-steps (Gaussian p→Student-t q→KL→gradient). 33→35 widgets.

Book (content/book/l5.js, 24→26 beats): turn-glove 4→5 paragraphs (matrix, f(x), objective,
count-vs-predict) + new scrolly beat climb-glove; climb-tsne 3→6 paragraphs (p/perplexity/q/KL/
gradient) + new scrolly beat climb-tsne-steps. Real numbers cited.

Deck (Lectures/05, 52→56): +GloVe co-occurrence (s30) +GloVe objective (s31) +t-SNE affinities
(s43) +t-SNE objective (s44) — native SVG, progressive reveal, HTML-overlay labels (no double-
paint), distinct colors, real numbers.

while the slides had: near-identical colors, overflow/overlap, and "everything appears at step

(LayerNorm bars / t-SNE dots / PCA rotation move per step = real progression). selftest proves
true defects fire AND false-positives stay silent.

  1.0 (no disambiguation). New gen: _research/gen_l6_contextual.py → data/l6-contextual.json.

  softmax saturates → divide to keep variance ~1; √d_k framed as a temperature (sharpness/
  entropy), cross-linked to InfoNCE τ.
- LayerNorm vs BatchNorm (depth-block-modern): per-token-across-features vs per-feature-
  across-batch; why BN breaks on variable-length/small-batch sequences; LN batch-independence.
- FFN-as-key-value-memory (depth-block-modern): Geva et al. 2021 expansion.

L5 (+1 beat → 24): new prose beat depth-pca-whitening between turn-dimred and climb-pca —

## c9e92ac · 2026-06-08 · L5/L6 B→A+: 8 architectural/geometric viz widgets (book) — SHOW the math, not just compute it

Addresses the "confident B" feedback: the lectures COMPUTED the math (numbers) but didn't
SHOW the architecture/geometry. 8 new scroll-step widgets (gate-safe, offline) wired into
the Book as new scrolly beats (L5 19→23, L6 27→31):

L5 (embeddings + dimred — the #1/#2 gaps):
- skipgram-net: the skip-gram NETWORK — one-hot → embedding matrix (= the lookup table)
  → projection → softmax. The architecture, not just the loss.
- embedding-domains: the SAME thing→tokens→vectors→shared-space across text/image/audio/
  protein (cross-domain, shown not told).
- pca-rotate: PCA AS ROTATION — 3-D cloud → eigenvector axes → rotate → flatten to 2-D
  (97.21% variance). You SEE covariance→eigenvectors→projection change the geometry.
- tsne-migrate: t-SNE AS MIGRATION — points migrate from scattered into meaning-clusters
  across iterations (the P7 caveat at the end).

L6 (transformers — the #3 gap; "what does LayerNorm DO?"):
- attention-geometry: attention as a geometric weighted-PULL toward whom a token heeds
  (blend ≈ the numeric output, within tol).
- layernorm-viz: what LayerNorm does — recenter (mean 5→0) → unit-scale (var 7.5→1) →
  onto the unit sphere → γ·+β.
- residual-stream: the residual highway — x + sublayer(x) accumulating, LayerNorm renorm √6.
- block-geometry: each sublayer RESHAPES the token cloud (blend → Add&Norm RMS=1 → FFN warp).

Data: reproducible _research/gen_l5_viz.py + gen_l6_viz.py (sklearn; real PCA/t-SNE,
deterministic toy nets); 8 new data/*.json; numbers consistent with existing data (PCA
97.21% kept distinct from dimred-projection's 37.7%; attention reused).

## 4f2a8fb · 2026-06-08 · Design L5 + L6 beat sheets (A+, full transformer in L6) — build-ready

Finalize the narrative beat sheets for the two neural-turn lectures per the agreed
design decisions:
- L5 "The Map of Meaning" (15 beats): symbols→geometry — distributional hypothesis,
  word2vec skip-gram, the king−man+woman≈queen analogy (exact cosines), GloVe, then
  dimensionality reduction (PCA exact, t-SNE/UMAP + the "t-SNE lies" caveat). New
  creature: the Cartographer. Gremlin cornered (not yet caged). Widgets: embedding-space,
  dimred-projection ← data/l5-*.json.
- L6 "The Council of Attention" (16 beats, FULL transformer block): "bank" static-vector
  problem → contextual embeddings → scaled dot-product attention (√d_k) → self-attention →
  multi-head → positional encoding → the full encoder block (Add&Norm + FFN, ×N) →
  contextual/[CLS] pooling → contrastive learning (InfoNCE, in-batch negatives) →
  anisotropy caveat → Lexical Gremlin CAGED (the L1→L6 payoff). New creature: the
  Council/Attention-Head. Widgets: attention-e2e, positional-enc, transformer-block,
  contrastive-space ← data/l6-*.json.

Both carry the machine `<!-- beats: … -->` header (beat-coverage contract) + widget/data
specs + the creature arc + acceptance criteria. Build order: L5 fully to A+, then L6.

## 5e910b6 · 2026-06-07 · Stabilization polish: unify all 28 dividers to one DOM + flex-aware figure cap

Closes the last backlog item (a prior pure-CSS attempt was reverted). Every
divider across the 5 decks now uses one shape — `.divider-content` + an optional
SIBLING `.viz-frame.fig-fit` + slide-notes (7 nested→sibling in L3, 7 already
sibling, 14 text-only). The figure is now FLEX-AWARE so it shrinks under tall text
instead of overflowing:
- divider = centered flex column; `.divider-content` flex:0 0 auto;
- the figure: `flex:0 1 auto; min-height:0; aspect-ratio:auto;
  max-height:var(--fig-max); width:var(--fig-w)` — `aspect-ratio:auto` UNPINS the
  height (the exact bug that sank the prior attempt), so flex-shrink can reduce it;
  the img keeps its ratio via object-fit:contain.
Tuned the lead-paragraph margin so the two text-heaviest L4 dividers' figures clear
the 150px TINY floor (134→158px, 138→162px) — no additive-gap blow-up this time.

Robustness: a future divider with a long heading/sub/recap now self-shrinks its
art rather than overflowing — no more per-divider --fig-max hand-tuning needed.

Gates: visual-gate HARD=0 / WARN=5 (no divider in any flag list), wbw-check 25/25,
token 0, facts 0, responsive 0, build clean.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

## 9857238 · 2026-06-06 · Stabilization: per-element auto-fit + formula self-fit; fix 4 KaTeX `<letter` bugs

Auto-layout — so a single overflowing element shrinks ITSELF, not the whole slide:
- deck.js: new fitToBox(el) scales only an element that genuinely overflows its
  own box (floor 0.7, +10px metric slack, no-op when it fits); runs before the
  global autoFitSlide on enter / step / details-toggle / katex:done / refit;
  auto-targets `.formula-stage`, `.step-formula`, and opt-in `.fit-box`.

  `.slide-body` in slides.css) — they previously overflowed freely. Eliminates 2
  overflow-v WARNs: L3 b-dial (1236px→0.79×, legible) and L4 CI (1173px→fits).
- visual-gate WARN 7→5; HARD=0; pixel-stable on the ~185 slides already at 1.0×.

Pre-existing KaTeX render bugs (a `<letter` inside \(...\) is parsed as an HTML
tag, breaking the span) — fixed to `\lt`: L3 `0<b<1` & `a<b`, L2 `0<p<1`,
L4 `\prod_{i<r}`. Found via the auto-layout pass.

Reverted (need deck markup unification — follow-up): CSS divider flex-shrink (two
incompatible divider DOM shapes) and e2e step-stacking (width trap). Floor stays
0.5 (97/282 slides already scale <1.0 — "too small" is widespread; this is a
partial fix, the deeper lever is splitting the densest slides + unified markup).
